- **technologies-cloud-security**: Cloud misconfigurations are the most common source of critical data breaches. The attack surface is: exposed credentials, misconfigured storage, overprivileged IAM, metadata service abuse, and serverless/container escapes. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-cloud-security))
- **technologies-docker-container**: Containers are frequently misconfigured in production. Attack surface: exposed Docker daemon API (direct RCE), privileged container escape, mounted host paths, weak seccomp/AppArmor, and Kubernetes RBAC misconfigurations. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-docker-container))
- **technologies-elasticsearch**: Elasticsearch is notorious for misconfigured public access — billions of records have been exposed via open Elasticsearch instances. Attack surface: no authentication by default (old versions), full data extraction, Kibana admin access, and Groovy/Painless script injection. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-elasticsearch))
- **technologies-firebase-firestore**: Security testing for Firebase applications. Focus on Firestore/Realtime Database rules, Cloud Storage exposure, callable/onRequest Functions trusting client input, and incorrect ID token validation. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-firebase-firestore))
- **technologies-frida-hooking**: Frida = inject JavaScript into running processes → hook functions, intercept calls, bypass checks, extract secrets at runtime. Works on Android, iOS, Linux, Windows, macOS binaries. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-frida-hooking))
- **technologies-gitlab-github**: Source code repositories are high-value targets. Attack surface: exposed private repos, hardcoded secrets in code/history, CI/CD pipeline injection (SAST bypass, token theft), IDOR in project access, webhook abuse, and numerous GitLab-specific CVEs. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-gitlab-github))
- **technologies-jenkins**: Jenkins is the most common CI/CD server in enterprise environments. Attack surface: unauthenticated Script Console (instant RCE), job configuration injection, credential store extraction, Groovy script execution, and numerous unpatched CVEs. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-jenkins))

- **technologies-memcached**: Memcached is a distributed memory caching system — no authentication by default. Exposure of Memcached leads to: full cache data extraction (may contain sessions, tokens, user data), cache poisoning, and UDP-based DDoS amplification. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-memcached))

- **technologies-mongodb**: MongoDB is frequently misconfigured with no authentication — exposing all databases publicly. Attack surface: no-auth by default (MongoDB &lt; 3.0), NoSQL injection in web apps using Mongoose/MongoDB driver, unrestricted network binding, and operator injection. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-mongodb))
- **technologies-nginx-apache**: Web server misconfigurations are among the most common findings. Attack surface: directory listing, alias path traversal, open redirects, server-side includes, proxy header abuse, and known CVEs. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/technologies-nginx-apache))
- **technologies-observability-stack-attacks**: Observability infrastructure (metrics, traces, logs) is routinely deployed without authentication because it's assumed to be internal-only. - **technologies-redis**: Redis is a high-value target: unauthenticated by default in older versions, and if accessible leads directly to RCE via cron job injection, SSH key writing, or webshell placement. - **technologies-supabase**: Security testing for Supabase applications. - **technologies-tomcat**: Tomcat is the most common Java servlet container.
